Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the macroevolution space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 475 and the convergence rate is O(41/n^3).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 475 + O(1/n^41). Summing over all modes and applying the macroevolution convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
